**Department:** Department of Medical Education and Research, Haryana

**Examination:** CET-2026 · Round 2 · as of 12.10.2026

**Source:** Provisional UG seat-allotment list after grievance redressal.

**Source rows:** 3390 across all UG courses.
**B.Sc. Nursing rows:** 2886; filtered by the final Round 2 “Allotted Course” field.

All source fields are retained, including date of birth and qualifying-exam percentage. The website table intentionally omits those two fields.

The source serial number is preserved as printed; it is not recalculated as a merit rank.
